# ξ (Riemann xi) — fact ledger
Snapshot 2026-09-26. Bare facts from the Lean corpus (formal/GaloisForLFunctions) and pipeline cards (docs/drafts/pipeline). Not a proof and not a history.

## 0. Notation, legend, core
### 0.1 Notation
- ξ(s)=½s(s−1)π^{−s/2}Γ(s/2)ζ(s). Ξ(t)=ξ(½+it). XiC(z)=Ξ_C(z)=ξ(½+iz). Λ=completed ζ.
- ρ = a zero of ξ (= a nontrivial ζ zero); β=Re ρ, γ=Im ρ, m_ρ = multiplicity. A "zero copy" q counts multiplicity.
- Centred coordinate α_q=γ_q=−i(ρ−½): real ⟺ ρ on the line; the partner 1−ρ̄ maps to conj α. z_ρ=−(ρ−½)^{−2}. Li/Arakelov coordinate w(ρ)=1−1/ρ.
- Theta source k (also written Φ): ∫_ℝ e^{wx}k(x)dx=ξ(½+w), equivalently Ξ(t)=∫k(x)e^{itx}dx.
- RH ⟺ β=½ for every zero. "Off-line" means β≠½. "Receiver" means a statement proved ⟺ RH (or ⟹ RH) that supplies no independent input. "Producer" means an unconditional proof of such a premise; none is known.
### 0.2 Legend
- Status: L = Lean-checked declaration; P = complete paper proof on a card; C = claimed, ledger-only, classical-cited, conditional or numerical; X = refuted or retracted. Combinations such as L+C or P+C mean mixed support.
- Lean source `Module:decl`: Module is a path under formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower (or under formal/GaloisForLFunctions), without .lean. HG/ = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/HTGeometry/. A bare module name cites the whole file.
- Card source `0B|1P|2F|3L|4R|5X/<path>` = docs/drafts/pipeline/{0-backlog|1-in-progress|2-fully-proven|3-formalized|4-publication-ready|5-failed}/<path>(.md).
- STATUS:n, killed:n and frontier:n = line n of the RH ledgers as read on 2026-09-25, before they moved (formerly under 1-in-progress: universal-law-field/STATUS.md, rh-killed-routes.md, rh-current-frontier-map.md; old copies at git 5ec49817f).
  - Current rewritten copies: docs/reports/STATUS.md, rh-killed-routes.md and rh-current-frontier-map.md. Line numbers there no longer match.
  - Entry IDs still match (F…, MUST-…, CANNOT-…, no-go-…); `1P/rh-killed-routes#no-go-N` refers to that ID.
- 2F/computations/* now lives at repo-root computations/*. The file results.md was deleted and exists only in git 5ec49817f.
### 0.3 Core (the 25 facts an agent needs first)
- ξ is entire of order 1 (maximal type), real on ℝ and on the critical line, with ξ(s)=ξ(1−s)=conj ξ(s̄) and ξ(0)=ξ(1)=½. ξ>0 on ℝ, and ξ(½)>0. Ξ is even and real, and Ξ restricted to ℝ is a Schwartz function. (§1, §2)
- The theta source k is positive, even and strictly log-concave, has all exponential moments, and satisfies k(x)≍e^{9∣x∣/2−πe^{2∣x∣}}. It continues to ∣Im z∣<π/4, which is a natural boundary. Ξ^{(2n)}(0)=(−1)^n·(positive moment), so Ξ″(0)<0. (§2.1, §2.2)
- The zeros are exactly the nontrivial ζ zeros. All lie in 0<β<1 and are closed under ρ↦1−ρ, ρ̄ and 1−ρ̄. Proved in Lean: ∣Im ρ∣>12.2, and there are no zeros for Re s>1−1/(58 log(∣Im s∣+4)); the corpus has no Lean declaration for the first ordinate 14.13. (§3)
- Riemann–von Mangoldt counting holds with O(log T) error. There are infinitely many zeros on the line (Hardy, in Lean). The on-line proportion ≥2/3 comes only through the pinned Zeta23 package (see §3.4 caveat; classical record 0.4173). S(T) is unbounded in both directions. (§3)
- ξ is hypertranscendental: it satisfies no algebraic ODE, and no family satisfying a bounded-shape ODE converges to it. Hypertranscendence carries no zero-location information. (§5, §8.9)
- ξ has no Euler product in the strip. Every finite Euler product is zero-free there with analytic roots of every order, so finite prime data are divisor-invisible. (§2.6, §8.10)
- De Bruijn–Newman: Λ≥0 is known, and RH ⟺ Λ=0. The flow moves zeros toward the line and carries no RH content beyond t=0. (§2.12, §8.19)
- Exact explicit formulas (Weil, Guinand–Weil, lag function Φ(L)=Σ_ρe^{L(ρ−½)}/(ρ(1−ρ))) and Hadamard pair laws hold unconditionally. For example, Re Σ_q γ_q^{−2}≤Σ∣γ_q∣^{−2}, with the gap equal to a reflection-defect energy. (§1.6, §2.15, §2.16)
- Unconditional smallness is proved: the orientation energy Σ(Im z_ρ)²<1/150000, and (Im z_ρ)²≤(32/49)(Re z_ρ)² at every zero. Smallness is not vanishing; vanishing of these quantities is RH. (§2.15)
- The corpus holds about 1,400 Lean RH equivalences. Nearly all are receivers of three types: (a) slack-free one-sided bounds, (b) positivity of a Weil/Hankel/Pick form, (c) pole-freeness of a prime-defined continuation. (§4.A)
- Sufficient conditions (⟹RH) all have open or RH-strength premises. (§4.B)
- Structural core: RH = triviality of the reflection ρ↦1−ρ̄ on the divisor, i.e. no free orbits (a two-torsion datum). Reflection-even real readers take equal values at ρ and its partner. Every localized or equivariant invariant sees only fixed points. (§8.1, §8.25, §9)
- No slack is available: Λ=0 exactly, and free-orbit counts are integers. A proof needs an exact identity or a height-uniform absolute bound (e.g. U(t)<2), not a lossy estimate. (§9.1, §8.3)
- Exact prime weights are load-bearing: deleting or perturbing one prime (p≥e^{18}) breaks Weil positivity. Any argument valid for perturbed prime measures or Beurling systems cannot prove RH. (§8.12, §9)
- Single-prime deformation principle: no inequality whose hypotheses survive an Euler-factor change at one prime can imply RH. (§8.10)
- Every structural property of ξ (FE, order 1, positivity, log-concavity, finite moments, the real divisor) is shared by functions with off-line zeros. (§7)
- Finite data never suffice: finite jets, moments, prime sets, windows, apertures and trace-class determinants are all excluded. The ξ divisor has genus one, so det₂ is needed and singular or cofinal limits are unavoidable. (§8, §9)
- Open: RH itself. There is no unconditional producer for any receiver. The sharpest open cores are listed in §10. (§10)
- Numerical statements are marked C and are never proofs. (§2.25, §8.28)

## 1. WHAT ξ IS (definition, coordinates, exact representations)

### 1.1 Definition and Lean aliases
- ξ(s):=s(s−1)/2·Λ₀(s)+½, Λ₀=completedRiemannZeta₀ (entire, mathlib); =s(s−1)/2·Λ(s) for s∉{0,1}; Λ(s)=Γℝ(s)ζ(s)=π^{−s/2}Γ(s/2)ζ(s)=Λ₀(s)−1/s−1/(1−s). ⟨L RiemannTower:xi; RiemannTower:xi_eq_half_mul⟩
- Aliases: RHTower.riemannXi=ξ exactly; RiemannGaussian.riemannXi(s)=s(1−s)Λ₀(s)−1=−2ξ(s) (same zeros and orders); xiFun = elementary·Γℝ·ζ (same zeros); ξ_spec(z)=−2ξ(½+iz); XiC(z)=ξ(½+iz) (some files ξ(½−iz); XiC even so same set up to sign of z); Xi(w)=ξ(½+w) in some files; Ξ(t)=Re ξ(½+it)=ξ(½+it) for real t. ⟨L RiemannXiZerosInfinite:riemannXi; RiemannGaussian/GaussianZetaCounting:riemannXi; GlobalParityWall:Xi; SuzukiWeilNormDefiniteness:xi_eq_XiC⟩
- Centred/spectral coordinate: z_ρ=γ_ρ=α_ρ=−i(ρ−½); ρ on the line ⟺ γ_ρ real; partner 1−ρ̄ ↦ conj γ_ρ; w=−(ρ−½)^{−2}=γ^{−2} (critical pair ↦ positive real γ^{−2}, FE pair {ρ,1−ρ} identified). Li/Arakelov coordinate w(ρ)=1−1/ρ: ∣w∣=1 ⟺ Re ρ=½, ∣w∣<1 ⟺ Re ρ>½, ρ↦1−ρ sends w↦1/w. ⟨L FEReciprocalFolding; C STATUS:7363; P 4R/archimedean-integrality-obstructions-manuscript⟩
- Descents: ξ(s)=D((s−½)²), D(w)=ξ(½+√w) entire, ord_{(s−½)²}D=ord_sξ for Re s>½; Q(z²)=ξ(½+iz) entire; Cassini source Ψ(u)=F(u−1), F(w)=ξ(½+i√w), Ψ(0)=ξ(3/2), zeros u=1+α_q² (one per FE pair). ⟨L UPlaneDescent:xi_eq_xiDescent; XiSquareDescentVerticalModulus; XiSquaredSeamCassiniSource⟩

### 1.2 Factorizations
- ξ=A·ζ, A(s)=½s(s−1)π^{−s/2}Γ(s/2) (archimedean factor), A(½+it)≠0 ∀t∈ℝ; A has order 0 in the strip, so ord ξ=ord ζ there; ξ=(s/2)Γℝ(s)·(s−1)ζ(s) on Re s>0. ⟨L C5JensenPrimeKernelClosure:XiC_eq_c5XiArchimedeanFactor_mul_riemannZeta; RouteRLocalRelativeAnomalyCancellationNoGo; HedenmalmThetaMobiusPoleUnitStrictTransverseCurvature⟩
- Critical line: ξ(½+iτ)=−((¼+τ²)/2)·∣Γℝ(½+iτ)∣·Z(τ) (Hardy Z real, even, Z(0)<0); Λ(½+it)=−2Ξ(t)/(t²+¼)=∣Γℝ(½+it)∣Z(t); Ξ(t)=½−((t²+¼)/2)Λ₀(½+it); Ξ(t)>0 ⟺ Z(t)<0. ⟨L HardyZ:xi_line_eq_scale_mul_hardyZ; HardyTheorem:completedRiemannZeta_critical_eq_Xi; HardyZThetaTrace:Xi_pos_iff_hardyZ_neg; ThetaCuspFourierXi⟩
- Eta form: in 0<Re s<1, c(s)η(s)=−2ξ(s), c(s)=s(1−s)Γℝ(s)/(1−2^{1−s}), η(s)=Σ[(2n+1)^{−s}−(2n+2)^{−s}]=(1−2^{1−s})ζ(s)=s∫e^{−st}dμ, μ=Lebesgue on ∪(log(2n+1),log(2n+2)]. ⟨L RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPositiveCriticalStripEtaCompletedSymmetry:pairedEtaCompletedXi_eq_riemannXi; …EtaInfiniteLaplaceMeasure⟩
- Burnol windows: ξ(w)=C_p(w)F_p(w), F_p=Γℝ(w)(p^w−1)ζ(w)/w (Mellin of window [1/p,1]), C_p=w²(w−1)/(2(p^w−1))≠0 in the strip. ⟨L BurnolAllPrimeCompletionWall; RouteRBurnolXiPrimeTwoCompletionLanding⟩
- Real ray (Bose): ξ(1+a)=(1+a)2^{−a}π^{−a/2}M(a)/Γ(a/2), M(a)=∫t^a/(e^t−1)dt=Γ(a+1)ζ(a+1). ⟨L BoseMellinCoordinate:xi_ofReal_one_add_eq_boseMellin⟩
- Squarefree split: ξ=[(s/2)Γℝ(s)ζ(2s)]·[(s−1)ζ(s)/ζ(2s)], first factor zero-free on Re s>½; E₁=(s−1)e^{P(s)} (P prime zeta) continues to Re s>½ with exactly ξ's orders there. ⟨L XiSquarefreePoleUnitRootReduction; XiPoleClearedFirstTraceRootReduction⟩
- Shift ratios: ξ(s)/ξ(s+2)=2π(s−1)/((s+1)(s+2))·ζ(s)/ζ(s+2); Θ_ω=ξ(½−ω−iz)/ξ(½+ω−iz) = archimedean·exp(convergent shifted Euler-log series) on Im z>½+ω; Mellin of Suzuki's h_ω is ξ(s−ω)/ξ(s+ω). ⟨P 5X/xi-shift-ratio-fixed-aperture-rh-no-go; L XiShiftEulerProductBridge; SuzukiCanonicalArithmeticMellin⟩
- Gaussian–Euler continuum: Φ_x(s)=½sπ^{−s/2}Γ(s/2)exp(L_x+A_x) zero-free on Re s>0, Φ_x→ξ locally uniformly on Re s>1 as x→0⁺ (not in the strip). ⟨L GaussianEulerContinuumRelativeFormation⟩
- Finite approximants: symmetric Euler–Maclaurin completed members →ξ uniformly on compact strip subsets at rate CN^{−σ}; completed Euler-deletion prefixes →ξ on 0<Re s<1; real Taylor polynomials of ξ_spec →ξ_spec locally uniformly. ⟨L FiniteMemberSteering; EulerDeletionCompletedChronology; RiemannGaussian/FiniteToEntireRealApproximation⟩

### 1.3 Hadamard product and log-derivative
- Genus one: XiC(z)=XiC(0)∏_q(1−z/γ_q)e^{z/γ_q} (multiplicity-exact, locally uniform, even); Ξ(x)=Ξ(0)Re P(x). ⟨L XiHadamardGrowth:XiC_eq_constant_mul_canonicalProduct; XiOrderedPrimeDeterminantNormalForm; XiLaguerrePolya:Xi_eq_center_mul_canonicalProduct_re⟩
- ξ'/ξ(s)=Σ_ρm_ρ[(ρ−½)^{−1}+(s−ρ)^{−1}] (absolutely convergent, no linear term); 2ξ'/ξ(s)=Σ_q[(s−ρ_q)^{−1}+(s−1+ρ_q)^{−1}]; XiC'/XiC(z)=½Σ_q[(z−γ_q)^{−1}+(z+γ_q)^{−1}]=−zΣ_q w_q/(1−z²w_q), w_q=γ_q^{−2}; X'/X(W)=Σ_ρW/(W²+α_ρ²); Σ_q(w²+γ_q²)^{−1}=ξ'(½+w)/(wξ(½+w)) for w>0. ⟨L XiExplicitFormulaLogDeriv:logDeriv_xi_eq_partialFraction; XiLocalZeroDensity:two_logDeriv_xi_eq_pair_tsum; XiHerglotzCriterion; XiRelativeResolventTrace; SquaredXiCompleteMonotonicity⟩
- Four-factor split: ξ'/ξ(s)=1/s+1/(s−1)−(log π)/2+ψ(s/2)/2+ζ'/ζ(s) wherever ζ(s)≠0 (Re s>0); on Re s>1 =1/s+1/(s−1)+Γℝ'/Γℝ(s)−ΣΛ(n)n^{−s} (explicit formula in log-derivative form). ⟨L XiLogDerivFactorization; RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiRealAxisCompletedLog; XiExplicitFormulaLogDeriv:xi_explicitFormula_logDeriv⟩
- Shift exposure: (ξ'/ξ)(s+2)−(ξ'/ξ)(s)−[1/(s+2)+1/(s+1)−1/(s−1)]=ΣΛ(n)(1−n^{−2})n^{−s} (>0 on real s>1); general a∈ℕ: archimedean parts cancel to Σ Λ(n)(1−n^{−a})n^{−s}. ⟨L XiShiftPrimeExposure; XiTransverseShiftPrimeDefectValueReflection⟩
- Taylor at 0 of −ξ'/ξ: P_k=Σm_ρρ^{−k} (k≥2 absolutely convergent); P_1=Λ₀(0)=(γ_E−log 4π)/2+1 is a Hadamard finite part (Σ1/ρ diverges absolutely). ⟨L XiHadamardFinitePart⟩
- Moment germ: −(XiC'/XiC)(z)/z=Σ_n μ_n z^{2n}, μ_n=Σ_ρm_ρ(−(ρ−½)^{−2})^{n+1} (real), = normalized Taylor moments of the continued −ζ'/ζ−A'/A source; converges at z=i/2 with Σμ_n(−¼)^n=C=2+γ_E−log 4π. ⟨L PrimeArchCentralMomentGerm; XiEndpointCompletedValue; XiEndpointCentralMomentDyadicBridge⟩
- Resolvent moments s_n(X)=Σ_q(α_q²+X)^{−(n+1)} are log-derivative jets of Q: n!m_n(y)=−2(dⁿ/duⁿ)(Q'/Q)(−y); d/dX s_n=−(n+1)s_{n+1}; heat bridge n!m_n(y)=∫tⁿe^{−yt}Θ(t)dt, Θ(t)=Σe^{−tγ_ρ²}. ⟨L XiShiftedHankelCassiniSourceJet; XiShiftedHardEdgeMomentDerivatives; XiShiftedHankelHeatResolventBridge⟩
- Laguerre identity: XiC'²−XiC·XiC''=XiC²·Σ_q(z−a_q)^{−2}; at real t the q-atom of Re Σ is ((t−γ_q)²−δ_q²)/((t−γ_q)²+δ_q²)²; Ξ'²−ΞΞ''=−Ξ²c₂, c₂=(−ξ'/ξ)'(½+it). ⟨L XiLaguerreZeroSumIdentity:laguerre_XiC_eq_tsum_inv_sq; XiLaguerreWindowPositivity; CompletedFluxLaguerreReader⟩
- Hadamard pair law: x=Re s−½>0, δ=Re ρ−½, y=Im s−Im ρ: Re[(s−ρ)^{−1}+(s−(1−ρ̄))^{−1}]=2x(x²−δ²+y²)/(((x−δ)²+y²)((x+δ)²+y²)); >0 ⟺ δ²<x²+y². ⟨L XiHadamardPairSignLaw; XiHadamardPairOrdinateIntegral⟩
- Poisson density: for a>½, Re ξ'/ξ(½+a+it)=(π/2)Σ_q[P_{a+Im γ_q}(t−Re γ_q)+P_{a−Im γ_q}(t+Re γ_q)]. ⟨L XiBoundaryPoissonDensity⟩

### 1.4 Theta / Fourier representations (ξ as a transform of positive data)
- Theta source k(x)=Σ_{n≥1}φ_{πn²}(x)=Σ_n n^{−1/2}h(x+log n), φ_λ(x)=2λu^{5/4}(2λu−3)e^{−λu}, u=e^{2x}; equivalently Φ(x)=Σ_n(4π²n⁴e^{9x/2}−6πn²e^{5x/2})e^{−πn²e^{2x}}; k>0 on (0,∞), even, Schwartz, holomorphic even on ∣Im∣<π/4; ∫_ℝe^{−izx}k(x)dx=XiC(z) for ALL z∈ℂ; Ξ(t)=2∫₀^∞Φ(x)cos(tx)dx. ⟨L HedenmalmThetaSourceSpectrumExact:thetaCuspFourierLaplace_eq_XiC_all; ThetaCuspComplexStrip; PrimeArchThetaFirstMoment:Xi_eq_thetaCuspCosineRepresentation; ThetaCuspSchwartzFourierXi; P 2F/theta/theta-prime-deletion-spectral-escape⟩
- Seed atom: first theta atom s₁(t)=2π(2πt⁴−3t²)e^{−πt²} has Mellin transform = archimedean factor A(s); ∫e^{wx}h=(w−½)Γ(w/2+5/4)π^{−w/2−¼}, zero-free on ∣Re w∣<½; ∫₀^∞seed=0 (source decays super-exponentially both ends); every atom has ∫e^{x/2}φ_λ=0, ∫e^{−x/2}φ_λ=−1. ⟨L EulerHedenmalmGammaStokesTransgression:mellin_thetaCuspSeed_eq_archFactor; ThetaCuspSeedZeroMean; P 2F/theta/theta-euler-critical-boundary-mass-escape⟩
- Tail forms: Φ=T''−T/4 with T(x)=Σe^{x/2}e^{−πn²e^{2x}}; Λ₀(½+it)=½∫h(u)e^{−iut/2}du, h(u)=e^{u/4}(θ(e^u)−1) even; Λ₀(s)=[𝓜θ⁺(s/2)+𝓜θ⁺((1−s)/2)]/2; Ξ(z)=½−(z²+¼)(M_z+M_{−z})/4, M_z=∫₁^∞y^{iz/2−¾}(θ(y)−1)dy entire. ⟨L XiCTaylorThetaTailCompletedDensityReflection; XiThetaCriticalTrace; RiemannGaussian/GaussianXiGrowth; P 2F/theta/theta-rational-cusp-boundary-law⟩
- Tilts: for ∣a∣<1, e^{ax}k is Schwartz with transform ξ(½−a+iw) (horizontal lines = real spectra of tilted sources); ∫w⁴∣ξ(½−a+iw)∣²dw=2π∫∣k_a''∣²; k-th jet: 𝓕[(−2πix)^kS]=Ξ^{(k)}. ⟨L ThetaCuspHawkingTiltedMirrorOpticalTheoremEndpoint; MobiusThetaInternalJetPhysicalGraph⟩
- Prime deletion on the source: k_S=∏_{p∈S}(1−p^{−1/2}T_{log p})k=Σ_{(n,∏S)=1}φ_{πn²}, transform ξ(½+w)∏(1−p^{−1/2−w}); translation gauge multiplies by M_μ(w). ⟨P 2F/theta/theta-prime-deletion-spectral-escape; 2F/theta/theta-all-sieve-seed-projector-translation-cone⟩
- Möbius–theta sources (built without ξ): transform ζ(m(½+iw))^{−1}·XiC^{(k)}(w) (∣Im w∣<1, Re(½+iw)>1/m); L² version a.e. on the line for m≥3. ⟨L MobiusThetaInternalJetSourceToXiLanding; MobiusThetaPhysicalSourceToXiLanding⟩
- Half-line: K(w)=∫₀^∞k e^{wu}du satisfies K(w)+K(−w)=ξ(½+w) (∣Re w∣<1); one-sided primitives P(w,x)+P(−w,−x)=e^{iwx}XiC(w); de Branges split E+E#=4z²XiC(z) with E=(z²+¼)Φ₊(z)−¼; ∣E∣=∣E#∣ at every strip zero. ⟨P 2F/causal-mobius-theta-boundary-criterion; L ThetaCuspInversionBoundaryValue; XiCTaylorThetaTailCompletedDensityDeBranges⟩
- Renewal/probabilistic forms (a=¼, c=θ(1)): Ξ(2iλ)=(a+λ)D(λ)+(a−λ)D(−λ); cosh factorization Ξ(2iλ)=2ac·e^{−C(λ)}cosh(η+S)/cosh η (prefactor zero-free on ∣Re λ∣<¼); amplitude–phase Ξ(2τ)=2c√(1/16+τ²)e^{−∫Qcos}cos[arctan 4τ+∫Q sin] (positive zeros = phase level π/2); Ξ(2z)=(c/2)∫e^{−t/4}cos(zt)g(t)dt, g a probability density (mean <2/3). ⟨P 2F/theta/theta-renewal-phase-cosh-factorization⟩

- de Bruijn–Newman: H_t(z)=∫₀^∞e^{tu²}Φ(u)cos(zu)du, H₀=ξ(½+iz)/8; Λ=inf{t: all zeros of H_t real}. ⟨C 4R/euler-functional-equation-independence⟩

### 1.5 Operator / de Branges representations
- Hilbert–Schmidt theta operator B with det₂(I+zB)=XiC(z)/XiC(0) ∀z; zeros ⟺ ker(I+zB)≠0; algebraic multiplicity = analytic order; singular metric pencil G(I+zB). ⟨L HedenmalmThetaSourceDeterminantIdentity; HedenmalmThetaMetricPencil⟩
- Annihilator ideal of O(ℂ)/XiC·O(ℂ) is (XiC); (s/2π)((s−1)/2π)Λ(s)=ξ(s)/(2π²). ⟨L DeningerArithmeticCharacteristic; DeningerPoissonZetaDuality⟩
- de Branges/Suzuki: E=XiC+iXiC', Θ=E♯/E, zero-function carrier i(1+Θ)/2=iXiC/E; E^ω(z)=ξ(½+ω−iz), Θ_ω unimodular on ℝ; Hermite–Biehler ⟺ ∣Θ_ω∣<1 on Im z>0; E(−i)=ξ(3/2)−ξ'(3/2). ⟨L RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiFiniteExpansion; SuzukiCanonicalShiftRatio; SuzukiDeBrangesDeficiencyKernel⟩
- Scattering: PSL(2,ℤ) φ(s)=ξ(2s−1)/ξ(2s) (each zero gives a pole at ρ/2 on the second sheet); Selberg zero kernel K_ρ(r) a Poisson pair, ≥0 for 0≤β≤1. ⟨C 1P/hb-obstacle40-working-log; L SelbergLedgerCeiling⟩
- Hedenmalm boundary problem: for ∣Im α∣<1 the theta-integral eigenfunction satisfies both endpoint conditions ⟺ XiC(α)=0 (unique normalized solution); multiplicity m ⟺ theta Fourier–Laplace moments of order <m vanish. ⟨L XiHedenmalmBoundaryValueProblem; XiHedenmalmThetaBorelHolonomy⟩

### 1.6 Explicit formulas (all unconditional)

### 2.6 Euler product / prime-side structure
- Finite prime data are divisor-invisible: multiplying ξ (or the theta source) by finitely many Euler factors/deletions, or changing finitely many prime phases, preserves every interior zero with multiplicity; multiplying ζ by exp(∫e^{−st}g) with g≥0 of finite σ-mass for σ>½ leaves zeros in Re s>½ unchanged. ⟨L PrimeDivisorAtomsNoGo:xi_divisor_prime_invisible; HG/FiniteGaugeTailCharge:finiteEulerGaugeRatio_ne_zero; BeurlingRHSensitivityThreshold:zeros_unchanged_of_mass_abscissa_le_half; P 2F/theta-prime-deletion-spectral-escape⟩

- Valuation ≥2 prime-log tail H(s)=Σ_pΣ_{k≥2}log p·p^{−ks} holomorphic on Re s>½ (cannot cancel valuation-one poles); prime-square layer of the mass current bounded; prime cube-and-higher tail absolutely bounded; only valuation one carries RH. ⟨L XiPrimeLogHigherValuationHalfPlane:differentiableOn_xiPrimeLogHigherValuation_halfPlane; XiMassPrimeSquareBoundaryResidue:exists_norm_xiMassPrimeSquareLayer_vertical_bound⟩

- Dyadic sharp prime source S(n)=4(1−1/√2)√n−2W(n): ΣS(n)n^{−s} continues to Re s>½ with simple poles exactly at ρ+½ for ALL ξ zeros (critical included), residue 2A(ρ+½)mult(ρ); negative-part abscissa ≤3/2 unconditionally, ∈[1+β*,3/2] under ¬RH; BV prime windows recover multiplicities. ⟨L CCMInverseTiltedDyadicPrimeTomography:normalizedInverseTiltedDyadicResidue_eq_multiplicity; CCMSharpPrimeWindowDivisorTomography:dyadicSharpPrimeSourceDivisorTomography_capstone; CCMSharpPrimeWindowMinimalPositiveCorrection:dyadicSharpPrimeSourceNegativePart_abscissa_le_three_halves; XiCCMMultiObservableDivisorUniversalityLaw:normalizedBVPrimeWindowResidue_eq_xiRightMultiplicity⟩

- Corrected shift values are injective in the shift set; prime logs ℚ-linearly independent; γ↦(2^{2iγ},3^{2iγ}) injective (one prime clock aliases ordinates). ⟨L XiShiftDivisorFaithful:completedPrimeSource_translate_independent; XiWeilCarrierBudget:linearIndependent_log_primesBelow_rat; XiHorizontalCollisionTwoPrimeCesaroCarrier:xiHorizontalPrimePhase_pair_injective⟩
- Each prime-local factor of Θ_ω is real >1 at imaginary anchors (prime factors individually non-contractive). ⟨L XiShiftEulerPrimeFactor:one_lt_eulerXiPrimeAnchorFactor⟩

- Critical-line prime amplitudes p^{−½} are neither summable nor square-summable; marked prime first-jet energies ≥(log X)²/4−O(log X) (never o(log²X), even on the line). ⟨L C5JensenPrimeKernelClosure:c5JensenPrimeKernelClosure_capstone; HedenmalmPrimeNormalSolderingCurvature:sum_hedenmalmMarkedPrime_firstJetEnergy_ge⟩

### 2.7 Reflection structure
- ξ^{(n)}(1−s)=(−1)^nξ^{(n)}(s), ξ^{(n)}(s̄)=conj ξ^{(n)}(s); at the partner of a zero ξ^{(n)}(1−ρ̄)=(−1)^n conj ξ^{(n)}(ρ); ∣ξ'(1−s)∣=∣ξ'(s)∣; ξ(1−s̄)=conj ξ(s). ⟨L RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPositiveCriticalStripEtaCompletedSymmetry:iteratedDeriv_riemannXi_one_sub; XiDerivReflection:deriv_xi_one_sub; DictionarySign:xi_lineReflect⟩
- Uniqueness of the symmetry: ξ(b−s)=ξ(s) ∀s ⟹ b=1; antiholomorphic reflection ξ(2a−s̄)=conj ξ(s) ⟹ a=½; ξ has no nonzero (real, imaginary or complex) period; Λ has poles at 0,1, no period, only reflection b=1, Λ(2−s)≠Λ(s). ⟨L SeamVerticalDecay:xi_entire_reflection_eq_fe; SeamVerticalDecay:xi_antiholo_axis_eq_half; SeamVerticalDecay:xi_not_periodic; SeamMeromorphicRigidity:completedRiemannZeta_not_continuousAt_one⟩
- Pair ledgers are even in δ: for a zero set stable under ρ↦1−ρ̄, ρ̄, each pair bracket φ(ρ)+φ(1−ρ̄) is even in δ=Re ρ−½ for every φ (no pair-summed ledger is first-order sensitive to displacement). ⟨L ZeroLedgerPairParity:pairLedger_even⟩
- FE transports log∣ζ∣ between reflected abscissae without information gain: log∣ζ(σ+it)∣−log∣ζ(1−σ+it)∣=(½−σ)log(t/2π)+O(1/t). ⟨C 4R/euler-functional-equation-independence⟩
- Clifford–Dirac: JDJ=−(D+1), C=D+½ anticommutes with J, (C+aJ)²=C²+a²; Mellin symbol a²+(w−½)² real at all zeros ⟺ RH. ⟨P 5X/xi-reciprocal-clifford-square-source-rh⟩
- Endpoint Ward law: endpoint-weighted Ward index I=2π·(off-line endpoint charge)∈[0,93π/1000); I=0 ⟺ RH. ⟨L XiWardEndpointSourceIndex:xiWardEndpointSource_globalLaw⟩
- Literal source executions have unit norm iff ∣z∣=1 (grade enters quadratically in source rank). ⟨L HG/LiteralSourceReflectionDeterminantCocycle:norm_complexCharacterEdgeReflectionBlockMultiplier_eq_one_iff⟩
- Round-ordered alternating ζ series (a odd, b even terms per round) converge iff a=b. ⟨L EtaArithmeticRoundConvergence:roundPrefix_converges_iff⟩
- Every reflection s↦b−s preserving ξ has Re b=1 with fixed point on the critical line; XiC(z)=conj XiC(−z̄) (jets transform with (−1)^k to the conjugate zero); XiC^{(k)}(−z)=(−1)^kXiC^{(k)}(z); ∣XiC(z)∣ depends on the horizontal displacement only through its absolute value; U(a,y)=log∣ξ(½+a+iy)∣ even in a. ⟨L SeamRealAxisGrowth:xi_reflection_center_re_eq_one; DeningerCompactOpenGradedRealJetUniversalityLaw:deningerCompactOpenXiJet_functionalEquationSigma; XiHedenmalmMultiplicityChain:iteratedDeriv_XiC_neg; SuzukiCanonicalShiftedXi:norm_XiC_eq_horizontal_abs; RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPointwiseChebyshevLogAverageLaplaceBoundaryHeatSelectedHalfStripXiLogNormReflection:shiftedRiemannXiLogNorm_neg_realCoordinate⟩
- Log-derivative reflection: (ξ'/ξ)(1−s)=−(ξ'/ξ)(s), (ξ'/ξ)(s̄)=conj; Re(ξ'/ξ)(½+it)=0 (so Re ξ'/ξ≥0 cannot be strict up to the line); D(s)=(ξ'/ξ)(s+2)−(ξ'/ξ)(s) satisfies D(−1−s)=D(s); finite shift divisors obey reflection laws with forced vanishing of derivatives at σ/2. ⟨L SeamFluxCompletion:logDeriv_xi_reciprocity; XiLogDerivSymmetry:logDeriv_xi_conj_xiLogDerivSymmetry; XiShiftDivisorValueReflection:logDeriv_xi_signed_reflection; XiShiftDivisorValueReflection:xiShiftDivisorLogDeriv_reflection⟩
- Descent: ord_{½}ξ=2·ord₀D; for critical ρ≠½, (ρ−½)² real ≤0 and ord D=ord ξ (FE descent gives no parity constraint on critical zeros); parity of ord ξ at Re s>½ = parity of D at (s−½)². ⟨L HalfChargeSeamCompat:xi_central_order_eq_two_mul; QuadrantSeamConservation:xiDescent_ne_zero_farField⟩
- Reciprocal traces: Σz_ρ^{−k} converges absolutely for k≥2; FE makes every odd trace (k≥3) vanish; k=2m+2 trace = S_m=Σ(z_ρ^{−2})^{m+1}; Re S_m>0 for m=0,1,2; raw traces T_k=Σm_ρρ^{−k} satisfy T_k=(−1)^kΣ_nC(n+k−1,k−1)T_{k+n} (e.g. Σ(n+2)T_{n+3}=0). ⟨L XiCenteredReciprocalLaurentParitySourceUniversalityLaw:summable_xiCenteredReciprocalLaurentCoordinate_pow; XiLaurentCalibratedReciprocalParityGapUniversalityLaw:xiLaurent_calibratedReciprocalParityGapUniversality; XiEndpointReciprocalTraceReflection:xiRawReciprocalPowerTrace_reflection⟩

- Eta reflection multiplier B(s): L(1−s)=B(s)L(s) with ∣B∣<1 for Re s<½, =1 ⟺ Re s=½, >1 for ½<Re s<1 (height-free); leading-moment FE at zeros compatible with off-line zeros. ⟨L RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPositiveCriticalStripEtaCompletionReflectionMultiplier:pairedEtaLaplaceReflectionMultiplier_eq_explicit; RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPositiveCriticalStripEtaLeadingLogMoments:pairedEtaLeadingLogLaplaceMoment_conjugatePartner⟩

- AFE/EM carriers: g_N=D_N+χD_N(1−·) completion Φ_N FE-even, real on the line; ∣ξ−Φ_N∣<∣Φ_N∣ ⟺ ∣ζ−g_N∣<∣g_N∣; symmetrized EM carrier error ≤∣s∣N^{−σ}/σ+∣χ∣∣1−s∣N^{σ−1}/(1−σ); decompleted residue (s−1)(ξ(s)−c)/A(s)→1−2c. ⟨L ZetaCarrier:completedGN_one_sub; SymmetrizedEMCarrier:two_zeta_sub_semCarrier_norm_le; XiUnitEulerPoleAffineSection:tendsto_translatedDecompletion_residue⟩

- Split residue: T(e,d)=Λ(1+e)Λ(ρ−d)Λ(ρ+d)Λ(2ρ−1−e) has no finite e→0 limit at a zero; regularized T−R/e has joint limit 0. ⟨L ThetaCuspHawkingBMXiReaderSplitResidue:orbit_split_residue_ne_zero; ThetaCuspHawkingBMXiReaderSplitResidue:regularized_joint_zero_limit⟩

### 2.8 Log-derivative ξ'/ξ: values, signs, bounds
- Symmetries/values: ξ'/ξ(1−s)=−ξ'/ξ(s), conj-symmetric, real on ℝ, ξ'/ξ(½)=0, ξ'/ξ(1)=Λ₀(1), ξ'/ξ(0)=−Λ₀(0); poles = zeros with residue ord (simple poles, (ξ'/ξ)' has exact order −2 at every zero); ζ'/ζ(½)=(γ_E+π/2+3log 2+log π)/2 = −(archimedean part at ½). ⟨L XiWardInteriorTraceSplit:logDeriv_xi_one_sub; SuzukiTorqueContinuationPole:meromorphicOrderAt_deriv_logDeriv_xi_eq_neg_two; SeamDivisionSourceSplit:seam_division_source_split⟩
- Real ray: Re ξ'/ξ(x)>0 for x>½, <0 for x<½; (ξ'/ξ)(½+w)/log w→½; w^j(d/ds)^j(ξ'/ξ)(½+w)→(−1)^{j+1}(j−1)!/2; 0<(ξ'/ξ)(½+x)<x(ξ'/ξ)'(½); (ξ'/ξ)'(½)=ξ''(½)/ξ(½)>0 = zeroth prime–Γ moment; (log b)/8≤(ξ'/ξ)(½+b)≤log b+1 beyond explicit bases. ⟨L AnharmonicChebyshevNoetherCasimir:logDeriv_xi_ofReal_re_pos; XiShiftedMomentFarRightAsymptotic:tendsto_scaled_iteratedDeriv_xiRayLogDeriv_shift; HedenmalmStokesCurvatureNormalizedFluxBudget:deriv_seamFluxCochain_one_half_re_neg; XiCriticalKernelSeparatedRealPacketPositivity:xiCent_ofReal⟩
- Unconditional positivity region: Re(ξ'/ξ)(s)>0 for Re s≥1; Re(ξ'/ξ)≥0 on Re s>½ with ∣Im s∣≤14 (every off-line zero has (Im ρ)²≥431/2) and for ∣Im s∣>4 with Re s≥1−1/(58 log(∣Im s∣+½)); Im(XiC'/XiC)≤0 for 0<Im z<½, ∣Re z∣≤218/25; −XiC'/XiC Herglotz on Im z>½; low-height band discharges at R=4, 161/40, 87/10, 218/25. ⟨L XiLogDerivLineOnePositive:re_logDeriv_xi_pos_of_one_le_re; XiLogDerivReLowHeight:re_logDeriv_xi_nonneg_of_low_height; XiHadamardSharpRegion:re_logDeriv_xi_nonneg_of_abs_im_le_four; PrimeArchExactOffLineQuartetAccretivity:continuedPrimeArchSourceRelativeTrace_im_nonpos_twoHundredEighteen_twentyFifths; XiHerglotzCriterion:im_logDeriv_XiC_nonpos_of_half_lt_im; PrimeArchEndpointFrequencyAccretivity:xiRelativeResolventTerm_lineConj_pair_im_nonpos_of_endpointBand⟩
- Locality: Re(ξ'/ξ)(s)≥0 if all zeros with ∣Im ρ−Im s∣<½ satisfy (Re ρ−½)²<(Re s−½)²+(Im s−Im ρ)² (criterion local in the ordinate; pair term positive once (Re s−½)²+(Im s−Im ρ)²≥¼); tail terms with ∣γ∣≥(∣Im s∣+1)/x+1 are ≥0 with no hypothesis, so finitely many low copies decide the sign; window-width transfer from any zero-free half-width; off-line height ≥H ⟹ Re ξ'/ξ≥0 on the box ∣Im s∣≤H−½. ⟨L XiLogDerivReLocality:re_logDeriv_xi_nonneg_of_near_zeros; XiHadamardPairSignLaw:re_inv_add_re_inv_lineConj_pos_of_far; XiHadamardTailNonneg:xiPairedResolventTerm_nonneg_tail; XiHadamardTailNonneg:tsum_low_le_two_mul_re_logDeriv_xi; XiLogDerivReWindowWidth:re_logDeriv_xi_nonneg_of_window_width; XiHadamardPairOrdinateIntegral:re_logDeriv_xi_nonneg_of_offLine_height⟩

### 2.16 Weil lag function Φ(L)=Σ_ρ e^{L(ρ−½)}/(ρ(1−ρ)) and lag pole series G(s)=Σ_ρ(ρ(1−ρ))^{−1}/(s−(ρ−½))
- a₁=Φ(0)=2+γ−log 4π; integer samples a_n=Φ(log n) exceed a₁−ε and are ≤−η infinitely often (Ω± of order √n); the sign of the prime error carries no zero information. ⟨L WeilLagFunctionOmega; C killed:4412⟩

- Φ₋=Φ₊−Φ is a nonnegative density with Laplace transform L[Φ₊]−G; Landau's theorem needs only analyticity of L[Φ₊] (no bound on ∣Φ∣). A pole ρ₀−½ with Re>σ₀ forces Re G(ρ₀−½−t)≤−Re w₀/t+C as t→0⁺. ⟨L WeilLagFunctionPositivePart:complexLaplace_oneSidedMeasure_zero_flip; WeilLagFunctionHerglotz:re_le_half_add_of_lagPoleSeries_re_nonneg⟩

Unconditionally Re G(s)≥0 on Re s>½; ½ is the exact wall of the termwise pair method; Φ(u)e^{−σ∣u∣} positive-definite at σ≥13/25 (RH ⟺ threshold 0). ⟨L LagPairThresholdSharp:pairNumerator_nonneg_forall_iff; LagPairThresholdSharp:re_lagPoleSeries_nonneg_half_of_hyperbolic; HyperbolicZeroFreeClosed:re_lagPoleSeries_nonneg_half; WeilLagFunctionHerglotz:integral_weilLagFunctionReal_damped_cos_nonneg⟩
- On-line zeros contribute Poisson terms Re w_ρ·σ/(σ²+(t−γ)²)>0 to Re G(σ+it); an off-line pair adds a signed lobe ∝4a²γ(t−γ) that t-averaging does not remove. Prime part P(u)=Σ_{n≤e^u}Λ(n)(e^{u/2}/n−e^{−u/2})≥0; the resulting 3–4–1 inequality is consistent with off-line zeros. ⟨C STATUS:5868; STATUS:5870⟩

### 2.17 Mollifier / mean values
- Unconditional gate: the mollified mean-square gate holds for every R>0 with some crude c (product AFE, Barban–Vehov), so all gate consumers are unconditional; gate constant floor: c<exp((5/3)R) is refuted cofinally (from zero counting). ⟨L ZetaGateProductAFEDyadicLaw:exists_zetaMollifiedMeanSquareGate_productAFE; MollifiedGateConstantFloor:not_zetaMollifiedMeanSquareGate_of_lt_exp⟩

### 2.19 Topology / cohomology / roots
- Local roots: around every point ξ has analytic n-th roots on small slit charts; deck class of local n-th roots at ρ₀ is exp(2πi·ord/n) (trivial at level 2^k ⟺ 2^k∣ord); far-right deck classes trivial. ⟨L DeckWindow:xi_exists_chart_roots; XiDeck:xi_deckClass⟩
- Equivariant roots: on open convex Ω stable under s↦1−s̄ with ½∈Ω and n even: Schwarz-equivariant n-th root ⟺ plain n-th root ⟺ n divides every zero order in Ω (uses ξ(½)>0); on reflection-invariant sets avoiding the line: equivariant ⟺ plain root on the right half. ⟨L EquivariantRootDissolution:xi_equivariant_root_iff_orders_dvd; EquivariantOfflineRegion:xi_equivariant_root_iff_offline⟩
- Windows: on a circle about ½+iτ₀ with ξ≠0 on it, ξ(c+re^{i(π−θ)})=conj ξ(c+re^{iθ}); an equivariant 2^k-th root of the boundary loop exists ⟺ 2^k divides the enclosed order sum and the on-line order sum over (0,τ₀+r) is even (equivalently Re ξ(½+i(τ₀+r))>0); small circles about ½ have roots at every level; level-2^{k+1} deck class equals exp(2πiL/2^{k+1}) ⟺ 2^k∣N (D=L+2N). ⟨L CreaseHolonomy:xi_window_tower_iff_ledger; RealRootTower:xi_circleLoop_equivariant; RealRootTower:xi_central_window_realTower; DiscWindow:xi_deckClass_eq_line_iff_right_dvd⟩
- Circle charges see only aggregate multiplicity mod 2^k (local charges can cancel); a charged boundary loop around a critical zero has a uniform sup-distance floor m>0 from all zero-winding loops. ⟨L XiDescentCircleAggregateCancellationUniversalityLaw:xiDescent_circle_aggregateCancellation; PrimeAFXichargeNoNucleation:exists_xiBoundaryLoop_zeroWinding_uniformGap⟩
- Towers: ξ and Λ carry coherent square-root towers on Re s>1; every finite Euler weld has analytic 2^k-th roots on Re s>0; all-level dyadic roots extend unconditionally to the symmetric de la Vallée Poussin cusp and the (strictly larger) Littlewood cusp (width ∝ log log∣t∣/log∣t∣); a square root of E₁ extends to a zero-free cusp; every such domain retreats to Re s=1. ⟨L WeldTowerLimit:weld_tower_survives_to_wall_extends_iff_rh; XiSymmetricZeroFreeCuspDyadicRoots:exists_xi_symmetricZeroFreeCusp_arithmetic_extensions_all_levels; XiSymmetricLittlewoodZeroFreeCuspDyadicRoots:exists_xi_littlewoodSymmetricZeroFreeCusp_arithmetic_extensions_all_levels; XiPoleClearedFirstTraceSymmetricZeroFreeCusp:exists_poleClearedFirstTrace_symmetricZeroFreeCusp_arithmetic_extension⟩
- Ladder normal forms: for each k, 2^k∣ord at every zero with Re ρ>½ ⟺ same for ½<Re ρ<1 ⟺ same with Im ρ≥0 ⟺ ξ has a Schwarz-equivariant analytic 2^k-th root on {Re s≠½}; k=1 ⟺ analytic square root on Re s>½. ⟨L RHCharacterizations:rhLadder_tfae; EquivariantRootDissolution:rhLadder_iff_equivariant_offline_root⟩
- Deninger quotient: multiplication by XiC is injective on O(ℂ); on H¹=O(ℂ)/XiC·O(ℂ), multiplication by (½+iz) has eigenvalue ½+iα ⟺ ξ(½+iα)=0; H¹≅Π_ρℂ^{m_ρ} (jet local–global: F divisible by XiC ⟺ F^{(k)}(z_ρ)=0 for k<m_ρ; all jet families realized). Meyer prime Koszul coordinate never surjective; each (ρ,p) gives a nonzero reporter. ⟨L DeningerEntireQuotientCohomology:XiC_eq_zero_iff_exists_deningerEntireTheta_eigenvector; DeningerArithmeticGlobalJetExact:deningerCompactOpenH1AllJets_bijective; FrankensteinMeyerPrimeKoszulGammaReporter:deningerMeyerXiPrimeGammaCharacter_eq_deltaS⟩

### 2.20 Differential-algebraic type (hypertranscendence and independence)
- ξ is hypertranscendental: no nonzero algebraic ODE over ℂ(s); same for ξ², XiC, XiC/XiC(0), exp(Q(s))ξ(s) (Q any polynomial), exp(ξ) (entire, nowhere zero); ξ,ξ',ξ'',… are algebraically independent over ℂ(s); any nonzero differential polynomial vanishes on ξ-jets only on an isolated set. ⟨L XiHypertranscendenceUnconditional:xi_hypertranscendental; PrimeIsingTreeFixedModalClosureNoGo:XiC_hypertranscendental; XiPainleveTauGaugeNoGo:xi_expPolynomialGauge_hypertranscendental; XiExponentialDeckChargeBlindness:xiExponential_hypertranscendental; XiJetFreeness:algebraicIndependent_xi_iteratedDeriv⟩

### 3.2 Symmetry, orbits, multiplicity
- Divisor invariant under s↦1−s, s↦s̄, s↦1−s̄ with multiplicity: ord equal on {ρ,ρ̄,1−ρ,1−ρ̄}; 2-adic valuation constant on each quartet. ⟨L XiHadamardFactorization:xi_divisor_one_sub; CentralReciprocalMoments:xi_divisor_conj; WeightMismatchRefutation:xi_orderAt_quadruple_eq⟩
- σ:ρ↦1−ρ̄ fixes a zero ⟺ Re ρ=½; off-line zeros form free 2-orbits; ρ↦1−ρ fixes no zero (ξ(½)≠0). Each ordinate slice {ρ: Im ρ=t} is finite, σ-stable; its σ-fixed part has ≤1 point; #slice = [½+it is a zero] + 2·#free orbits; #distinct zeros at ordinate t is odd ⟺ ξ(½+it)=0. ⟨L DivisorGaloisNorm:supportPartner_eq_self_iff; DivisorSliceFixedPart:xiSliceFixed_subsingleton_unconditional; BoundaryMapImage:sum_ncard_xiSlice; C STATUS:7149⟩
- Horizontal multiplicity h(t) has parity of ord_{½+it}ξ; an off-line zero forces h(Im ρ)≥2 and ord²≤ord·(H−ord); a zero alone at its height is critical and simple. ⟨L HorizontalFibreParity; HorizontalSingletonZeroFiber:xiHorizontalSingletonZero_re_eq_half; XiReflectionSelfIntersectionCharge⟩
- Ball parities about ½: off-line multiplicity even; off-both-axes multiplicity divisible by 4; secular odd moments Σ_{∣ρ−½∣<R}m_ρ(ρ−½)^{2m+1}=0; conj M_n=(−1)^nM_n. Strip window identity: total order = on-line + 2·(Re>½ order), so mod-2 window totals never see off-line zeros. ⟨L SecularOddMoments; WindowLadder:stripWindow_parity⟩
- ord_{½}ξ is even (=2·ord₀ of D(w)=ξ(½+√w)); actually ξ(½)≠0. ⟨L ReflectionParity:xi_central_order_even; HalfChargeSeamCompat⟩
- Odd-order critical zeros exist above every height (Hardy sign changes; not every order is even); simple critical zeros exist above every height, with Z(t)=0, Z'(t)≠0. ⟨L GlobalParityWall:exists_odd_order_line_zero_gt; ArithmeticJointJetExclusion:exists_high_simple_critical_xi; EulerDeletionHorizontalContact:exists_high_simple_center⟩
- Multiplicity mechanics: local analytic symmetry τ fixing ρ with τ'(ρ)≠1 ⟹ ord≥2; τ'=−1 ⟹ even order; a simple zero admits no such τ; XiC(z)/(z−γ) nonzero at γ ⟺ simple (k-fold quotients obey a Jordan recurrence); de Bruijn kernel-row vanishing forces a multiple zero. ⟨L SeamOfflineReflection:xi_zero_ramified_imp_not_simple; XiHedenmalmMultiplicityChain; PositivityForcesMultipleZeros⟩

- Eta identities at every nontrivial zero: Σ[(2n+2)^{−ρ}−(2n+3)^{−ρ}]=1; second-difference series =−1; log-moments M_k(ρ)=0 for k<m, M_m≠0. ⟨L RiemannGaussian/RiemannXiSuzukiPositiveCriticalStripEtaHorizontalDefectGapArithmetic; …LeadingLogMomentGapDefect⟩

- Q_L-Dirichlet series Σ_nQ_L(½log n)n^{−s} continues meromorphically to Re s>1 with simple poles exactly at ρ+½ for Re ρ>½, residue 2m_ρc_L(ρ) (normalized residue = multiplicity); holomorphic there ⟺ RH; negative-part abscissa is −∞ under RH and exactly 1+δ* otherwise. ⟨L CCMRectangularManuscriptCapstone; CCMRectangularFullObservableDivisorTomography; CCMRectangularNegativeDirichletAbscissa⟩

## 4. CRITERIA

### 4.A RH EQUIVALENTS (X ⟺ RH; Lean-proved unless marked)
- Meta: the corpus holds ≈1,400 Lean-level RH equivalences. Nearly all are receivers: they rephrase Re ρ=½ at every zero, or they take the zero as a parameter. None is known to hold unconditionally. The genuinely source-side ones (prime sums, Möbius, explicit-formula values, prime–Γ moments, theta source) are listed first in each group. ⟨C STATUS:1476,1521,1850⟩
- Recurring reduction: every known RH-equivalent source quantity is either (a) a one-sided bound with no slack, (b) a positivity of a Weil/Hankel/Pick form, or (c) analyticity/pole-freeness of a prime-defined Dirichlet continuation on Re s>1. ⟨C §4.A⟩

#### 4.A.1 Zero-free forms and width laws (Re ρ≤½+α; α=0 is RH)
- Basic: RH ⟺ every ζ zero has Re ρ≤½ ⟺ ζ≠0 on Re s>½ ⟺ on ½<Re s<1 ⟺ on 0<Re s<½ ⟺ (s−1)ζ(s)≠0 on Re s>½ ⟺ ζ(s)/ζ(2s)=Σ∣μ(n)∣n^{−s}≠0 on Re s>½ ⟺ every ζ zero has (2Re ρ−1)Im ρ=0. ⟨L ReflectionCocycleClass:riemannHypothesis_iff_forall_zero_re_le_half; FormationPotentialCriterion:riemannHypothesis_iff_zetaZeroFreeRight; Sieve/InvZetaRegular:riemannHypothesis_iff_zetaResidueUnit_ne_zero; SquarefreeFixedCurrentLocalization:riemannHypothesis_iff_squarefreePartition_ne_zero; MFreeTwinNoGo:riemannHypothesis_iff_dyadicMFreeZetaRung_zero_ne_zero; XiNodalDefectSuperselection:everyZetaZeroNodalDefectPure_iff_riemannHypothesis⟩
- Width α: all Re ρ≤½+α ⟺ ψ(x)−x=O_ε(x^{½+α+ε}) ⟺ H(t)≤M(t)+Ce^{αt} (Suzuki hinge sum; ∣Re ρ−½∣≤ε form) ⟺ Wold residual R(N)=O(N^{−c}) ∀c<½−α (also √N-window means) ⟺ Poisson trace vertical type ≤α ⟺ inverse-tilted source analytic on Re s>1+α ⟺ critical-Mellin defect analytic on Re u>α−½ ⟺ rectangular negative energy finite above 1+α ⟺ ∂_a∣ξ(½+a+ib)∣²≥0 ∀a∈(α,1) (horizontal monotonicity; prior art) ⟺ Suzuki: Θ_ω meromorphic inner ∀ω>α. ⟨L CCMSharpPrimeWindowSquareEnergyWidthCollision:xiZeroHalfPlaneBound_of_chebyshevErrorPowerSpectrum; SuzukiPsiRateIdentity:screwPrimeSumRate_iff; XiHeatCassiniWoldResidualNaturalWidthSpectrum:xiZeroHalfPlaneBound_iff_woldNatResidualPowerSpectrum; XiHeatCassiniWoldResidualNaturalSqrtMeanSpectrum:xiZeroHalfPlaneBound_iff_woldNatResidualSqrtMeanSpectrum; XiSpectralApertureConservationUniversalityLaw:xiPoissonTraceVerticalTypeAtMost_iff_xiZeroHalfPlaneBound; CCMSourceDivisorEnergySpectrum:ccmQuantitativeSourceDivisorEnergySpectrum_capstone; XiRealTransverseQuartetSurgeryMatroidInvariance:xiThetaNormalOrientationAbove_iff_zeroHalfPlaneBound; SuzukiLagariasMeromorphicInner:suzuki2012_zetaZeroFree_iff_forall_theta_meromorphicInner⟩
- Square aperture: σ₂ (abscissa of Σ(W(m²)−2(1−1/√2)m)₊²m^{−s}) = 1+4 sup(Re ρ−½) off RH; RH ⟺ σ₂≤1 ⟺ cumulative square energy O_δ(M^{1+δ}). ⟨P 5X/route-i-square-aperture-positive-excess-dual-bound⟩
- Lag exponent: Φ(L)=e^{θL}·(bounded); RH ⟺ θ=0; fixed-width zero-free region β≤1−c ⟺ θ≤½−c (any lag exponent below ½ would be a new unconditional theorem). ⟨C STATUS:5706⟩

- Canonical all-negative Helson sheet ζ(2s)/ζ(s) pole-free on ½<Re s<1 ⟺ RH (no source-faithful exponent-zero gauge changes this). ⟨C killed:7907⟩

#### 4.A.2 Real zeros / critical line (structural forms)
- RH ⟺ every ξ zero has Re ρ=½ ⟺ ξ≠0 on Re s<½ (mathlib's s≠1 clause redundant) ⟺ all zeros of XiC(z)=ξ(½+iz) real ⟺ Im z_q=0 ∀ copies ⟺ ρ+ρ̄=1 ∀ρ ⟺ 1−2Re ρ∈ℤ ∀ρ. ⟨L ZetaXiZeroCorrespondence:riemannHypothesis_iff_xi_zero_re_eq_half; XiSuperchargeHarnackDefect:riemannHypothesis_iff_XiC_zeros_real; XiCriticalLineIntegrality:riemannHypothesis_iff_forall_exists_int_one_sub_two_re⟩

- Squared-seam dyadic tail: RH ⟺ Re z_q^{2^k}≥0 ∀q ∀k≥6 (z_q=(1+α_q²)^{−1}; lower rungs k≤4 unconditional). ⟨L XiSquaredSeamTailFromSixtyFour:xiSquaredSeamDyadicTailFromSix_iff_riemannHypothesis⟩

#### 4.A.4 Laguerre–Pólya / Jensen / Hermite–Biehler / Bezoutian
- RH ⟺ Ξ∈LP ⟺ all Jensen polynomials of Ξ(t+·) hyperbolic ∀t,d ⟺ power-sum Hankel matrices H_d(t)⪰0 (LP translation invariance; shift depth redundant). Lean: RH ⟹ LP; LP membership of XiC ⟹ RH. - Weil criterion: RH ⟺ explicit-formula quadratic form ≥0 on all tests; Weil functional = archimedean form − prime form, so RH ⟺ the uniformly definite archimedean form dominates the prime form; ind₋(W_ξ)=½#(off-line zeros) (critical orbit PSD block, real pair signature (1,1), quadruple (2,2)); operator form H_W=−Δ_prime+V_arch⪰0 on ℓ²(smooth numbers). ⟨C STATUS:391; L ArchimedeanPairingDefinite:weil_as_domination; P 4R/euler-functional-equation-independence; C 0B/weil-positivity-operator⟩

### 5.1 Differential/algebraic class

- exp(Q)·ξ hypertranscendental ∀ polynomial Q ⇒ ξ ≠ exp(Q)·E for any entire differentially algebraic E; no Painlevé III(D₆) tau carrier (any monodromy/gauge) equals ξ; Planat degree-2 bridge conjecture false. ⟨L XiPainleveTauGaugeNoGo:no_entire_differentiallyAlgebraic_expPolynomialBridge_to_xi⟩
- ξ'/ξ not holonomic (no linear ODE with polynomial coefficients); ξ not DA of any order. ⟨L XiLogDerivNonholonomic:xi_logDeriv_not_holonomic; XiJetFreeness:xi_not_differentiallyAlgebraicOfOrder⟩
- Σ_j P_j(d/ds)(ξ'/ξ)(s+a_j) (distinct a_j, P_j≠0) hypertranscendental over ℂ(z); every finite differential-translation filter of ξ'/ξ (e.g. (ξ'/ξ)(s+h)−(ξ'/ξ)(s), h≠0) is non-DA; z↦ξ(−iz) hypertranscendental over ℂ(z). ⟨L XiTranslationOrbitFreeness; XiUpperEndHypertranscendence⟩
- ξ + (finite complex Gibbs sum Σ_a e^{−E_a s}) stays constant-coefficient differentially transcendental; ξ ≠ any finite exponential sum; c(z+(1+z²)e^{Ξ}) hypertranscendental. ⟨L XiJetFreeness; XiHypertranscendenceUnconditional:xi_ne_finiteMassClockSignal⟩
- ζ satisfies no nonzero finite linear shift equation Σ_{j≤N} r_j(s)ζ(s+j)=0, r_j∈ℂ(s); ζ and affine pullbacks ζ(σ(1+s)) hypertranscendental. ⟨L ArithmeticLDifferenceHolder:riemannZeta_not_hasFiniteOrderLinearShiftEquation; HG/EulerQGammaHypertranscendence⟩
- ξ satisfies no 2nd-order linear ODE ⇒ Stieltjes electrostatic derivation of zero identities unavailable. ⟨C STATUS:7229 CANNOT-415⟩
- ξ (order 1) is not a Poincaré function φ(λz)=f(φ(z)) of transcendental entire DA f (those have infinite order); finite-order ⇒ any entire composition ξ=F∘G lies in Pólya's zero-order-outer regime. ⟨P 2F/universal-first-order-entire-cascade-unit-cost; C 1P/positive-order-composition-depth-vs-differential-order⟩
- ζ zero set contains no infinite vertical AP anywhere in the strip (Putnam et al.); with Ostrowski (DA ∧ multiplicative ⇒ divisor = union of vertical APs) ζ is not DA∧multiplicative. ⟨C 1P/goldilocks-divisor-rigidity⟩

### 5.2 Symmetry/periodicity
- ξ has no nonzero period (real, imaginary, complex); the only reflection s↦b−s preserving ξ is b=1; the only anti-holomorphic axis symmetry is Re s=½; Λ has no nonzero period and no Λ(2−s)=Λ(s). ⟨L SeamVerticalDecay:xi_not_periodic,xi_entire_reflection_eq_fe,xi_antiholo_axis_eq_half; SeamMeromorphicRigidity⟩

- FE+conjugation give a Real (class AI) structure: antiunitary T with T²=+1 fixing the line, swapping off-line mirror pairs ⇒ no Kramers-forced even multiplicity. ⟨P 1P/prime-covering-descent-index-divisibility-rh⟩
- No g:ℝ→ℂ with XiC(z)/XiC(0)=g(Re z). ⟨L SourceHorizontalRiggedKreinDeterminantCollapse:no_realProfile_normalizedXiC⟩

- One negative value locates a zero: Re(ξ'/ξ)(s)<0 at Re s>½, ξ(s)≠0 ⟹ ∃ zero ρ with (Re s−½)²+(Im s−Im ρ)²≤(Re ρ−½)². ⟨L XiLogDerivReFalsification:exists_zero_disk_of_re_logDeriv_xi_neg⟩

### 8.3 Integrality-anchor and individuation laws
- Zero individuation: no unconditional arithmetic identity observes an individual zero (each is a functional of the pair-even smoothed zero density); fixed-kernel positivity certificates resolve off-line zeros only to archimedean density ν(t)=(1/2π)log(t/2π) (the log t of every ceiling is the Γ-phase derivative); first-order sensitivity to δ exists only through half-plane cuts, alignment singularities or amplification. ⟨P 5X/zero-individuation-law-two-completion-producer⟩
- Integrality anchor: every zero-locating theorem (Hadamard–dlVP, Weil, Deligne, Lee–Yang, Kaczorowski–Perelli) contains a discrete invariant squeezed between an archimedean bound and gap 1; no real inequality on a continuous class containing ζ can force an off-line count to 0 (slack ≥log t per integer); no corpus invariant meets the anchor requirements (convolution powers, knapsack Betti numbers, window counts, Toeplitz indices, Iwasawa invariants, Kloosterman integrality, CM averages all fail); an off-line indicator integer is a receiver. Both terminal doors (𝔽₁ integrality; direct sub-unit anchor) are currently RH-equivalent receivers. ⟨P 5X/integrality-anchor-law; 3L/escape-multiplicity-offline-indicator-lean; C STATUS:4159⟩
- Affine rescaling of ζ preserves each zero's relative displacement within its strip (no amplification). ⟨L MertensOddWindowKnapsack:relativeDisplacement_affine⟩
- Reflection-even real readers are blind: any real-valued reader even under ρ↦1−ρ̄ (norm, energy, signature, Gram determinant, index, capacity, positivity) takes equal values on a zero and its partner (maps from a 2-torsion group to ℝ vanish). The partner's angular term jumps 0→1 at the line, so no functional continuous in the zeros detects RH (separating invariant is integer-valued); near-line off-line zeros are not small perturbations of Cauchy-kernel functionals. ⟨C STATUS:6976; STATUS:7317; STATUS:7302⟩
- Scaling degree: bumps are scale-invariant; pointwise averages (L^p, mollified moments, smoothed counts) have degree 1, only sup/TV/count have degree 0; ‖bump‖_p∝δ^{1/p}, ‖bump‖²_{H^s}≍δ^{1−2s}, so L^∞ and H^{½} are the only scale-invariant norms and H^{½} misses L^∞ by one log; critical-exponent (bubble) technology needs a variational problem whose critical points are the divisor. ⟨C STATUS:7398; STATUS:7467; STATUS:7489⟩

### 8.4 Counting / density / capacity
- Integer vs real: counts bounded by real-valued estimates lose ≥log t slack per integer; density bounds N(σ,T)≪T^a log^B T cannot certify N(σ,T)=0; verification to height X plus monotonicity says nothing above X; FE places each off-line quartet at one ordinate so it cannot raise the least off-line ordinate; a per-window collision-charge estimate below the quantum 2 is needed (estimates after dividing by the total count do not imply RH). ⟨L XiHorizontalCollisionChargeEndpoint:riemannHypothesis_of_eventually_horizontalCollisionCharge_lt_two; C 0B/terminal-classification-two-doors; P 4R/euler-functional-equation-independence; C 5X/verified-height-amplification⟩
- Displacement mass and free-orbit count: each bump of U has mass 2δarctan(R/δ)<πδ but height 1, so criteria reading displacement mass (∫N(σ,T)dσ, Littlewood's lemma, mollified moments, smoothed counts) cannot decide RH; free-orbit count ≤ Cauchy-weighted local density ≍C log(height) decides nothing — only a height-uniform absolute bound U<2 forces vanishing. ⟨C STATUS:7336; STATUS:7437⟩
- Jensen/angular capacity: on-line and off-line zeros contribute the same [1/π+o(1)]R log R; all zeros are within ½ of the real axis in the Ξ coordinate. Count/density of real Ξ zeros cannot give RH (one missing conjugate pair already gives a bounded annihilator of the real-zero system). ⟨P 2F/theta/theta-tail-divisor-capacity-threshold; 2F/theta/theta-endpoint-harmonic-completeness⟩

### 8.11 Lag / Weil-lag / prime-error routes
- Any finite uniform B in ∣X−1−Σ_{n≤X}Λ(n)log(X/n)∣≤B√X implies RH (optimal constants are not a prerequisite). - Smooth Littlewood identity: detecting one zero needs the mean of log∣ζ∣ to precision O(1/T₀), pair-even density inputs give only T₀^{−c}; means of log∣ζ∣ and Laguerre sums control zeros only at density level o(T). ⟨P 5X/zero-individuation-law-two-completion-producer; C 0B/terminal-classification-two-doors⟩

- Inner-band (Laguerre-at-γ) route is circular: the only anchor making the remaining inverse-square sum small is pointwise on-line density near γ. At half density p^{−½} is neither summable nor square-summable (no ℓ¹/ℓ² completion of first-order Jensen-root prime perturbations). ⟨P 5X/anchor-law-inner-band; L C5JensenPrimeKernelClosure:not_summable_sq_c5CriticalPrimeAmplitude⟩
- Quartic Jensen polynomials cannot be certified from independent dispersion bounds (tolerance ≍1/b diagonally, ≍1/b² transversally); curvature-infimum bounds on Φ are zero in the log variable; for d=o(n) hyperbolicity is unconditional (no individuation) and finite model matching stops below d≍n log n. ⟨C STATUS:5652; STATUS:5654; STATUS:5656⟩
- Laguerre margin, theta-Bezoutian diagonal and oblique sector conditions all reduce to real-part conditions on ξ′/ξ (one off-line zero makes its own atom negative; source arithmetic required). The factorial normalization 1/(m+n)! is not a PSD multiplier (2×2 det −½; block det −7/360) and exceeds 1/√((2m)!(2n)!) off-diagonal, so raw prime-side moment positivity is no evidence for the Jensen criterion. Weight δ²/(δ²+τ²)^k gives δ^{2−2k} at the zero's own ordinate: only k=1 can decide. ⟨C STATUS:6613; STATUS:6877; STATUS:6899; STATUS:7356⟩

- Doubling is free: ξ^e·u has all orders even if e even, an odd order if e odd (even-copy presentations carry parity for free); any nondegenerate symmetric K-self-adjoint form doubles to a symplectic K⊕K form; e-copy determinants (cusps, Fricke pairs, Weil components) give no parity constraint — the theta-multiplier Mayer transfer operator has e=2 (AII†/Kramers door closed). ⟨L GlobalParityWall:xi_copies_parity_dichotomy; HG/SymplecticDoublingFree:exists_symplectic_selfAdjoint_doubled; P 5X/metaplectic-transfer-operator-kramers; C killed:6921⟩

- Free orbits are invisible to equivariant invariants: for ℤ/2 acting by ρ↦1−ρ̄, free orbits are Tate norms/coboundaries (a finite cocycle is a coboundary iff its coefficient at every fixed label is even; Ĥ¹(C₂,ℤ^{(I)}⊗sgn)=(ℤ/2)^{fixed labels}); Tate cohomology, Smith theory, signature, Fredholm/Toeplitz index, Krein inertia, Witt/Hasse class, spectral flow and Maslov index are localized invariants (Atiyah–Segal–Quillen) and compute only critical zeros; genus/ambiguous-class formulas compute one bit per height (the fixed part is a subsingleton per slice); RH is the vanishing of the explicit coboundary N(1_{Re<½})=1_{Re≠½}, which cohomology discards. ⟨L HG/ReflectionTateAnomaly:reflectionTateCocycle_is_boundary_iff_fixed_even; ReflectionTateConservativeClassification:isReflectionTateConservative_iff; IntegralOrbitTateExtension:integralUnitExtension_tate_separates_fixed_free; P 5X/faithful-reflection-tate-conormal-localization-rh; 5X/genus-programme-retired; C STATUS:7077; STATUS:7093; STATUS:7108; STATUS:7418⟩

- Euler-deletion raw self-area and cross-modulus carry at the first 12 zeros take both signs (no positivity producer); a rank-two ordered-area law is strongly disfavoured; an off-line zero at height T moves Gram G_N by ≤N²log T/(πT), so finite Gram PSD checks never outrun the verified height. ⟨C STATUS:5317; 1P/euler-deletion-mixed-area-rank; STATUS:5714⟩

## 9. MUST (proved necessary features of any RH proof / any object carrying ξ's zeros)

### 9.1 Global principles (distilled from all no-gos)
- Exactness, no slack: Λ=0 exactly under RH (ξ sits at the de Bruijn–Newman threshold); integrality of free-orbit counts removes slack; RH must come from an exact identity/marginality or slack-free statement in which Dirichlet series and FE act together (Hamburger rigidity), not from a size estimate; no inequality toward RH can carry a uniform margin. ⟨C STATUS:7164 CANNOT-411; STATUS:6748 MUST-389; C killed:…⟩
- Absolute constants: through the free-orbit count/potential, need sup_t U(t)<2 uniformly in height; height-dependent bounds of any strength give nothing. ⟨C STATUS:7437 CANNOT-437⟩
- Every checkable property of ζ/ξ is either satisfied by deformations with off-line zeros or already equivalent to RH; a proof must construct a class (with a pairing) containing ζ as one member whose deformation space is neither everything nor a point. ⟨C STATUS:6768 MUST-390⟩
- Must use the Euler product / literal integer coefficients (a_n=1): FE, Γ, polar part, positivity, growth, Λ≥0, entirety each fail alone; Davenport–Heilbronn/Epstein share FE and have off-line zeros; any mechanism must fail for Beurling systems with off-line zeros satisfying the same clauses. ⟨C 0B/realization-problem; 5X/riemann-bifiltered-source-ancestry-purity; L GaussHodgeWeilSymmetryInsufficiency⟩
- Pre-divisor: the decisive law must be proved on source data (theta/prime/Euler–Γ) before specializing to ξ zeros; admissibility (compactness, positivity, normality, properness, bounded work, integrable potential) must be derived, not imposed — imposing it at zero fibres is an RH-equivalent endpoint; every cost/norm/topology must be fixed by source data first. ⟨C STATUS:1259,1296,1326,1345,3535,3654⟩
- Excluding every single off-line zero is required: zero-density with any sparse exceptional set is not RH; partitions of zeros need independent class definitions + exhaustive coverage + a proof per class. ⟨C STATUS:302,303⟩
- Unbounded parameter: every known criterion needs an unbounded parameter (support/order/heat time/aperture); no finite collection of strict inequalities continuous at the theta source k can certify RH (k is on the boundary of the real-rooted class). ⟨P 2F/theta/theta-derivative-dark-divisor-packets; C 1P/harmonic-invisibility-and-the-one-over-delta-law⟩
- Cross-zero coupling: atomwise positivity/bounds are already equivalent to RH (affine atomwise trace positivity, atomwise squared-Euler charge, per-zero budgets); a noncircular proof must use cancellation/coupling between different zero orbits. ⟨L XiShiftedAtomwisePositivityNoGo:xiShifted_atomwiseAffine_nonneg_iff_riemannHypothesis; XiSquaredEulerApertureDiagonalControlObstruction⟩
- Same-fibre, first-order orientation: the FE supplies only cross-fibre exchange ρ↔1−ρ̄ (a cross-fibre unitary exists for every displacement); reflection-pair cancellation is unconditional and loses orientation; a zero-locating relation must be same-fibre, first-order, and retain reflection-odd data (cannot be recovered after positive/projective quotient). ⟨P 1P/frankenstein-theta-prime-translation-plucker-anomaly; 1P/complete-resolution-reflection-pair-equicontinuity-firewall; C STATUS:1265⟩
- Retain typed data: multiplicity, reflection orientation, prime/depth labels, horizontal displacement Re ρ−½ must be carried (not total mass/norm); transformations forgetting real parts/multiplicities must recover them by a proved identity; ordinate-only self-adjoint realizations are achievable without RH. ⟨C STATUS:305,426,1268,5198⟩
- Singular limits necessary: any construction of the full critical divisor from fixed-aperture almost-periodic/bounded readers must pass through a singular limit (diverging reader norms or leaving every fixed weighted-Wiener aperture); an operator determinant with exact ξ divisor needs a non-trace-class source; a ξ divisor horizon (idempotent) must arise by singular/nonmultiplicative completion. ⟨P 2F/route stc/stc-physical-ordinate-transport-singularity; 2F/route stc/stc-mertens-trace-class-xi-divisor-barrier; C STATUS:4765⟩
- Exactness of prime data: Weil positivity holds only for exact positions log n and weights Λ(n)/√n; deleting/inserting/perturbing one prime weight forces a crossing; prime-side bounds surviving single-prime deletion (Chebyshev capacities, 0≤Λ≤log, averages) cannot prove Weil positivity at radius ≥½log p+8. ⟨L WeilPrimeMeasureRigidityCountable:countableFamily_nonneg_iff; WeilSinglePrimeDeletionUniform:deletedRHS_re_neg_of_large; WeilInsertionAnyWeight⟩
- The exponent ½ carries the content: lag/Weil producers must move the exponent from ½ to 0 at once (no partial credit), may lose constants but not the exponent; boundedness of the lag function by any M gives RH. ⟨L WeilLagFunctionCriterion:riemannHypothesis_iff_weilLagFunction_bounded; C STATUS:5664–5706⟩
- Resolution vs coincidence: excluding small real-part displacement (resolution; one log power short) and excluding exact ordinate coincidence of two zeros (codimension/transversality) are equivalent statements but different targets. ⟨C STATUS:7033 CANNOT-402⟩

### 9.2 Multiplicity / simplicity side-conditions
- Many strong mechanisms also force simplicity (must then prove it): positive continuous Hodge star on O(ℂ)/XiC·O(ℂ); symmetric-operator realizations (a multiple zero = nonzero Jordan step at a real eigenvalue); local unit-phase germ exists iff simple critical zero; Möbius–Laplace half-decay; prescribed-rate energy bounds; positive first-jet exclusion radii. ⟨L DeningerArithmeticJordanObstruction:xiSupportMultiplicity_eq_one; XiHedenmalmSymmetricMultiplicity:no_symmetric_sourceQuotient_realization_of_multiple_xi_zero; ArithmeticUnitPhaseRigidity:unitPhaseGerm_iff_simple_critical_zero⟩

- Even-multiplicity (parity) mechanisms need a canonical square root of local Euler factors (nonlinear, non-family); Kramers-type structure must be restricted to Re s>½ (s-independent cocycles cannot). ⟨P 5X/arithmetic-symplectic-descent; 5X/metaplectic-transfer-operator-kramers⟩
- Finite-head zero motion: must retain multiplicity m and phase exp(i Im ρ log N); vanishing prime-weighted energy is measured from the limit zero, not the line. ⟨L ArithmeticFiniteZeroDisplacement; ArithmeticPrimeEnergyTransport⟩

### 9.3 Operator / Hilbert–Pólya requirements
- Any self-adjoint K with ξ(½+iz)=H·det₂(I−zK) (H zero-free) has a_n=1/γ_{⌊n/2⌋}, a_n·π(n+1)/log(n+1)→1, Σ_{n<N}a_n~(log N)²/(2π), K∈S_q ∀q>1, K∉S₁, K∉weak-S₁, exact Schatten abscissa 1, minimal regularized-determinant genus 1. ⟨L XiDeterminantIdealConsequences; XiDeterminantSingularValueTransfer:xiDeterminant_approxNumber_exactXiDeterminant⟩

- Graded finite approximants: normalized charpolys of Even⊕Odd-graded operators converging to Ξ force eventually balanced graded dimensions (Ξ(0)≠0); with a graded radical removed, r_e=r_o+1 (one-dimensional radical must be even). ⟨L CCMGradedIndexDeterminantBalance⟩
- Hilbert–Pólya carriers must be: jet-sensitive (Jordan chains), scale-invariant only modulo a discrete subgroup (full scale invariance gives all positive reals), all-prime/noncommuting/singular or unbounded; a Suzuki-type limit must use two extension angles with meromorphic convergence off the pole divisor. ⟨C killed:7939; C 4R/euler-functional-equation-independence; 5X/suzuki-holomorphic-gauge-characteristic-limit⟩

- Completion index must be a family/local-length invariant (determinant-line torsion with multiplicity) with source provenance and a no-escape theorem; pointwise Fredholm index erases kernel–cokernel additions. ⟨P 5X/…/arithmetic-levinson-completion-index-law⟩

### 9.4 Positivity / Weil / Hankel requirements
- Reflection (Hankel) positivity must be supplied as new data (a reconstructed Hilbert space with self-adjoint generator); it cannot be derived from free translation positivity; a reflection-positivity carrier needs abundant 2-torsion strictly between the principal-divisor group of ℤ and its square-class quotient. ⟨C STATUS:6917,6956 MUST-393/398⟩
- All Weil negativity is conjugation defect: a negative witness must make the test transform differ at ρ and 1−ρ̄ by more than the total sampling energy; any producer must control exactly the defect sum. ⟨L SuzukiScrewFormConjugationDefect⟩
- Every v with Weil form Q(v)≥0 lies at squared distance ≥E/2 from the orientation vector v_E (∥v_E∥²=E, RH⟺E=0); repairs need ≥E of reflection-fixed transverse mass. ⟨L XiHurwitzOrientationSharpNonnegativeConeDistance; XiHurwitzOrientationBoundaryTransverseCompensation⟩
- The horizontal exponent cannot be diluted: any pointwise-summable nonneg channel cover of the negative Weil tail has aggregate abscissa ≥1+β*. ⟨L CCMSourceDivisorEnergySpectrum⟩
- Lag positivity is marginal: under RH the lag Toeplitz form has spectral infimum 0 on every window (no gap); geometric producers need an intersection pairing on principal divisors of Spec ℤ with values Φ(log(n/m)) and Hodge-index inequality Φ(0)≥Φ(L), tight along geometric progressions; the integer lag inequality a_n≤a₁ has zero margin infinitely often along every b^m. ⟨L WeilLagFunctionRecurrence:integerLag_law; C STATUS:5710⟩
- Negative Hankel witnesses for −Ξ'/(tΞ) must have degree ≥2 and coefficients of both signs; atomwise positivity already ⟺ RH. ⟨L ThetaGibbsSteinSourceMinor:forall_primeArchSourceTraceHankel_linear_nonneg_stein⟩
- Laguerre proofs must carry quantitative off-line separation: margin ≥0 at an off-line ordinate needs 2/δ² ≤ remaining resolvent energy. ⟨C STATUS:6605 MUST-382⟩
- Jensen–Pólya RH information sits at d≳n (below the saddle threshold, where no asymptotic model exists); the perturbative wedge is RH-blind; degree-d Jensen content is single-orbit hyperbolicity, not averaged. ⟨C STATUS:5658; 5X/combination-mode-baker-reducibility⟩
- Deformations preserving real zeros need real zero velocity −K(z₀)/H'(z₀) at each real simple zero. ⟨L HG/AffineSimpleZeroMotion⟩

### 9.5 Theta-source requirements
- Retain the singular negative-cusp boundary charge ½ (finite-prime sources never converge in L¹(e^{x/2}dx)); cannot exchange the Euler limit with the critical Mellin integral; retain the second (reflected −½) boundary observation; control moving spectral height and the full complex boundary family w↦∫e^{wx}k. ⟨P 2F/theta/theta-euler-critical-boundary-mass-escape; 2F/theta/theta-all-sieve-seed-projector-translation-cone; 2F/theta/theta-prime-deletion-spectral-escape⟩

- Riemann–von Mangoldt spectral mass in the theta Fourier quotient is carried by coupling between distinct frequency pairs: a reality proof must orient global cross-frequency coupling. ⟨P 3L/theta/theta-cdf-crossfrequency-rvm-capacity⟩
- Dyadic/square-root producers of Ξ must be signed/complex (not probability laws). ⟨L ThetaTiltXiInfiniteDivisibilityNoGo⟩

### 9.6 Prime / mollifier / density requirements
- Mollified mean-square gate constant must be ≥exp(5R/3) (R>0); all RH strength must come from −∫log∣Ψ(½+it)∣; an (L)-producer must beat Jensen (E log∣ζΨ∣² strictly below log E∣ζΨ∣²); only the full sum of both AFE halves plus cross term is O(1). ⟨L MollifiedGateConstantFloor; ZetaOffLineFirstMomentMollifierCredit; C STATUS:5259,5648⟩
